Despite this progress, these models frequently suffer from limitations caused by human reporting biases inherent in textual knowledge, leading to understanding discrepancies between machines and humans. To bridge this gap, we introduce an additional modality to enrich the reasoning capabilities of PLMs. We propose Imagine (Machine Imagination-based Reasoning), a novel zero-shot commonsense reasoning framework that supplements textual inputs with visual signals from machine-generated images. Specifically, we enhance PLMs with the ability to imagine by embedding an image generator directly into the reasoning pipeline. To facilitate effective utilization of this imagined visual context, we construct synthetic datasets designed to emulate visual question-answering scenarios. Through comprehensive evaluations on multiple commonsense reasoning benchmarks, we demonstrate that Imagine substantially outperforms existing zero-shot approaches and even surpasses advanced large language models. These results underscore the capability of machine imagination to mitigate reporting bias and significantly enhance the generalization ability of commonsense reasoning models

</details>


### [37] [Bidirectional Curriculum Generation: A Multi-Agent Framework for Data-Efficient Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05120)
*Boren Hu,Xiao Liu,Boci Peng,Xinping Zhao,Xiaoran Shang,Yun Zhu,Lijun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出双向课程生成框架，通过多智能体生态系统动态生成数据（增加或简化问题难度），建立闭环反馈机制，在数学推理任务中以更少的训练样本实现更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型数学推理能力的提升依赖于大规模数据集，但数据效率仍是关键瓶颈。标准课程学习（从简单到复杂）存在样本利用效率低下的问题：即使模型存在基础推理缺陷，仍盲目增加难度，导致在无法解决的问题上浪费计算资源。

Method: 提出双向课程生成框架（Bidirectional Curriculum Generation），核心包括：1）多智能体生态系统模拟自适应教学策略；2）闭环反馈机制动态生成数据，既可以通过增加问题难度来挑战模型，也可以通过简化问题来修复特定推理缺陷；3）基于最优节奏定理（Optimal Pacing Theorem）优化学习轨迹。

Result: 显著超越基线方法，在使用更少训练样本的条件下实现更优的数学推理性能。

Conclusion: 该框架通过双向数据生成策略确保模型在每个阶段仅消耗最有效的训练数据，有效解决了课程学习中盲目递增难度的低效问题，为数据高效的大语言模型训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: Enhancing mathematical reasoning in Large Language Models typically demands massive datasets, yet data efficiency remains a critical bottleneck. While Curriculum Learning attempts to structure this process, standard unidirectional approaches (simple-to-complex) suffer from inefficient sample utilization: they blindly escalate complexity even when foundational gaps persist, leading to wasted computation on unsolvable problems. To maximize the instructional value of every training sample, we introduce a novel Bidirectional Curriculum Generation framework. Unlike rigid trajectories, our multi-agent ecosystem mimics adaptive pedagogy to establish a closed feedback loop. It dynamically generates data by either complicating problems to challenge the model or, crucially, simplying them to repair specific reasoning failures. This mechanism ensures that the model consumes only the most effective data at any given stage. Grounded in the Optimal Pacing Theorem, our approach optimizes the learning trajectory, significantly outperforming baselines while achieving superior reasoning performance with substantially fewer instruction samples.

</details>


### [38] [GCAgent: Enhancing Group Chat Communication through Dialogue Agents System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05240)
*Zijie Meng,Zheyong Xie,Zheyu Ye,Chonggang Lu,Zuozhu Liu,Zihan Niu,Yao Hu,Shaosheng Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GCAgent系统，将LLM驱动的对话智能体从一对一扩展到多人群聊场景，通过Agent Builder、Dialogue Manager和Interface Plugins三个模块实现娱乐性和实用性对话智能体。实验表明该系统评分达4.68，用户偏好度提升51.04%，真实部署中消息量增加28.80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群聊是在线社交平台的重要形式，但常受限于活跃度不足和管理困难。虽然近期大型语言模型(LLMs)在一对一对话中表现出色，但其在多人会话场景中的无缝集成仍缺乏探索。

Method: GCAgent系统包含三个核心模块：1) Agent Builder用于根据用户兴趣定制智能体；2) Dialogue Manager负责协调对话状态和管理智能体调用；3) Interface Plugins通过三种工具降低交互障碍。系统支持娱乐性和实用性两类对话智能体。

Result: 实验评估中，GCAgent在各项标准上平均得分4.68，比基线模型提升51.04%的用户偏好度。在超过350天的真实部署中，消息量增加28.80%，显著提升了群聊活跃度和用户参与度。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一个将LLM对话智能体从单聊扩展到群聊场景的实用方案。GCAgent系统通过三个紧密集成的模块（Agent Builder、Dialogue Manager、Interface Plugins）有效提升了群聊的活跃度和用户参与度。实验表明该系统在评分和用户偏好方面均优于基线模型，真实部署350天内消息量增加28.80%，证明了其在实际应用中的有效性。

Abstract: As a key form in online social platforms, group chat is a popular space for interest exchange or problem-solving, but its effectiveness is often hindered by inactivity and management challenges. While recent large language models (LLMs) have powered impressive one-to-one conversational agents, their seamlessly integration into multi-participant conversations remains unexplored. To address this gap, we introduce GCAgent, an LLM-driven system for enhancing group chats communication with both entertainment- and utility-oriented dialogue agents. The system comprises three tightly integrated modules: Agent Builder, which customizes agents to align with users' interests; Dialogue Manager, which coordinates dialogue states and manage agent invocations; and Interface Plugins, which reduce interaction barriers by three distinct tools. Through extensive experiment, GCAgent achieved an average score of 4.68 across various criteria and was preferred in 51.04\% of cases compared to its base model. Additionally, in real-world deployments over 350 days, it increased message volume by 28.80\%, significantly improving group activity and engagement. Overall, this work presents a practical blueprint for extending LLM-based dialogue agent from one-party chats to multi-party group scenarios.

</details>


### [39] [X-RAY: Mapping LLM Reasoning Capability via Formalized and Calibrated Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05290)
*Gao Tianxi,Cai Yufan,Yuan Yusi,Dong Jin Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: X-RAY是一个可解释的推理分析系统，通过校准的、经过形式验证的探针来映射大型语言模型的推理能力，将推理能力建模为可提取"结构"的函数，包括约束交互、推理深度和解空间几何等形式属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽然取得了不错的性能，但它们的推理能力仍然难以理解。现有的评估主要强调任务级准确率，往往将模式匹配与推理能力混为一谈。

Method: X-RAY通过形式工具生成具有受控结构变化的探针，通过形式校准和验证实现增量结构信息的精确分离。将推理能力建模为约束交互、推理深度和解空间几何等形式属性的函数。

Result: 发现LLM推理存在系统性不对称性：模型对约束细化（附加条件缩小解空间）相对稳健，但对解空间重构（修改解流形的底层结构形式）急剧下降。经过校准的形式探针可以区分在标准基准上看起来无法区分的模型，并揭示结构上可解释的失败模式。

Conclusion: 该框架是无污染的，支持推理模型的训练和测试，为理解和评估LLM的推理能力提供了新的形式化方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve promising performance, yet their ability to reason remains poorly understood. Existing evaluations largely emphasize task-level accuracy, often conflating pattern matching with reasoning capability. We present X-RAY, an explainable reasoning analysis system that maps the LLM reasoning capability using calibrated, formally verified probes. We model reasoning capability as a function of extractable \textit{structure}, operationalized through formal properties such as constraint interaction, reasoning depth, and solution-space geometry. X-Ray generates probes via formal tools with controlled structural variations, enabling precise isolation of incremental structural information through formal calibration and verification. We evaluate state-of-the-art LLMs on problems ranging from junior-level to advanced in mathematics, physics, and chemistry. Our analysis reveals a systematic asymmetry in LLM reasoning: models are relatively robust to constraint refinement, where additional conditions shrink an existing solution space, but degrade sharply under solution-space restructuring, where modifications alter the underlying structural form of the solution manifold. Moreover, calibrated formal probes differentiate models that appear indistinguishable on standard benchmarks and reveal failure modes that are structurally interpretable rather than opaque. Beyond evaluation, our framework is contamination-free and supports the training and testing of reasoning models.

</details>


### [40] [STRUCTUREDAGENT: Planning with AND/OR Trees for Long-Horizon Web Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05294)
*ELita Lobo,Xu Chen,Jingjing Meng,Nan Xi,Yang Jiao,Chirag Agarwal,Yair Zick,Yan Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出STRUCTUREDAGENT框架，通过层次化规划和结构化内存解决大型语言模型在复杂长视野web任务中的决策问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有web智能体在复杂长视野任务中表现不佳，原因包括：1）上下文记忆有限，难以跟踪历史；2）规划能力薄弱；3）贪婪行为导致过早终止。

Method: 提出层次化规划框架STRUCTUREDAGENT，包含两个核心组件：1）在线层次化规划器，使用动态AND/OR树进行高效搜索；2）结构化内存模块，跟踪和维护候选解以改善信息搜索任务中的约束满足。框架还产生可解释的层次化计划，便于调试和人工干预。

Result: 在WebVoyager、WebArena和自定义购物基准上的实验表明，STRUCTUREDAGENT在长视野web浏览任务上相比标准基于LLM的智能体提升了性能。

Conclusion: STRUCTUREDAGENT框架有效解决了现有web智能体面临的内存、规划和贪婪行为问题，为复杂长视野任务提供了一种有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled agentic systems for sequential decision-making. Such agents must perceive their environment, reason across multiple time steps, and take actions that optimize long-term objectives. However, existing web agents struggle on complex, long-horizon tasks due to limited in-context memory for tracking history, weak planning abilities, and greedy behaviors that lead to premature termination. To address these challenges, we propose STRUCTUREDAGENT, a hierarchical planning framework with two core components: (1) an online hierarchical planner that uses dynamic AND/OR trees for efficient search and (2) a structured memory module that tracks and maintains candidate solutions to improve constraint satisfaction in information-seeking tasks. The framework also produces interpretable hierarchical plans, enabling easier debugging and facilitating human intervention when needed. Our results on WebVoyager, WebArena, and custom shopping benchmarks show that STRUCTUREDAGENT improves performance on long-horizon web-browsing tasks compared to standard LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [41] [WebChain: A Large-Scale Human-Annotated Dataset of Real-World Web Interaction Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05295)
*Sicheng Fan,Rui Wan,Yifei Leng,Gaoning Liang,Li Ling,Yanyi Shang,Dehan Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WebChain是最大的开源人工标注网页轨迹数据集，包含31,725条轨迹和318k步骤，采用视觉、结构、动作三重对齐的多模态监督方式。通过可扩展的标注流程获取复杂高价值任务数据，提出Dual Mid-Training方法将空间定位与规划解耦，在WebChainBench及其他公开GUI基准上取得最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1) 网页智能体研究缺乏大规模高质量人工标注数据；2) 现有合成方法遗漏复杂高价值任务；3) 网页智能体领域缺乏可复现的研究基准和严格评估体系

Method: 1) 构建包含31,725条轨迹和318k步骤的WebChain数据集；2) 提出视觉-结构-动作三重对齐的多模态监督框架；3) 设计可扩展的数据采集流程确保复杂任务覆盖；4) 提出Dual Mid-Training训练策略，将空间定位与规划解耦分别训练；5) 构建WebChainBench评估基准

Result: 在WebChainBench及多个公开GUI基准上取得最先进性能

Conclusion: WebChain数据集和Dual Mid-Training方法为构建和评估可扩展的新一代网页智能体提供了数据基础和训练范式

Abstract: We introduce WebChain, the largest open-source dataset of human-annotated trajectories on real-world websites, designed to accelerate reproducible research in web agents. It contains 31,725 trajectories and 318k steps, featuring a core Triple Alignment of visual, structural, and action data to provide rich, multi-modal supervision. The data is collected via a scalable pipeline that ensures coverage of complex, high-value tasks often missed by synthetic methods. Leveraging this dataset, we propose a Dual Mid-Training recipe that decouples spatial grounding from planning, achieving state-of-the-art performance on our proposed WebChainBench and other public GUI benchmarks. Our work provides the data and insights necessary to build and rigorously evaluate the next generation of scalable web agents.

</details>


### [42] [Ailed: A Psyche-Driven Chess Engine with Dynamic Emotional Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05352)
*Diego Armando Resendez Prado*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个"人格×心理"框架，通过音频信号链（噪声门、压缩器/扩展器、五段均衡器、限幅器）重塑象棋引擎的走子概率分布，模拟人类棋手的心理波动（压力vs过度自信）。实验在12,414局对局中验证，无需搜索，不依赖特定引擎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有象棋引擎虽然超越人类水平，但缺乏人类特有的行为模式——如压力下的失误。传统引擎无法捕捉人类棋手的心理波动和个性差异。

Method: 将行为变异分解为两个组件：1) 人格(Personality)——静态预设，决定引擎性格；2) 心理(Psyche)——动态标量ψ_t∈[-100,+100]，每步后根据五个局面因素重新计算。这两个组件输入音频信号链，实时调整走子概率分布。

Result: 两种训练数据量相差2800倍的概率源均显示一致的单调梯度：压力到过度自信间top-move一致率差异约20-25个百分点。过度自信时与原生Maia2一致率达66%；压力下竞技得分从50.8%降至30.1%。

Conclusion: 该框架通过信号链而非底层模型产生行为变异，呈现出类似人类" tilt"和过度自信的模式，但研究未包含人类被试验证。

Abstract: Chess engines passed human strength years ago, but they still don't play like humans. A grandmaster under clock pressure blunders in ways a club player on a hot streak never would. Conventional engines capture none of this.
  This paper proposes a personality x psyche decomposition to produce behavioral variability in chess play, drawing on patterns observed in human games. Personality is static -- a preset that pins down the engine's character. Psyche is dynamic -- a bounded scalar ψ_t \in [-100, +100], recomputed from five positional factors after every move. These two components feed into an audio-inspired signal chain (noise gate, compressor/expander, five-band equalizer, saturation limiter) that reshapes move probability distributions on the fly. The chain doesn't care what engine sits behind it: any system that outputs move probabilities will do. It needs no search and carries no state beyond ψ_t.
  I test the framework across 12,414 games against Maia2-1100, feeding it two probability sources that differ by ~2,800x in training data. Both show the same monotonic gradient in top-move agreement (~20-25 pp spread from stress to overconfidence), which tells us the behavioral variation comes from the signal chain, not from the model underneath. When the psyche runs overconfident, the chain mostly gets out of the way (66% agreement with vanilla Maia2). Under stress, the competitive score falls from 50.8% to 30.1%. The patterns are reminiscent of tilt and overconfidence as described in human play, but I should be upfront: this study includes no human-subject validation.

</details>


### [43] [PACE: A Personalized Adaptive Curriculum Engine for 9-1-1 Call-taker Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05361)
*Zirong Chen,Hongchao Zhang,Meiyi Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PACE是一个个性化自适应课程引擎，旨在辅助9-1-1接警员培训，通过概率 belief 建模、 learning/forgetting 动态追踪和情境 bandits 推荐来平衡新技能习得与已有技能巩固，相比现有方法可加快19.50%达到胜任水平，提高10.95%最终掌握度，与专家教学判断一致率达95.45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 9-1-1接警员培训需要掌握超过1000项相互依赖的技能，涉及多种事件类型和协议细节 全国性劳动力短缺已导致培训能力紧张，而有效的教学仍需培训师根据每个学员不断发展的能力进行个性化调整，这种个性化需求在当前实践中无法规模化实现

Method: 1) 在结构化技能图上传播证据以加速诊断覆盖；2) 使用上下文 bandits 选择针对学员准备弥补的差距的情境；3) 维持对学员技能状态的概率信念；4) 建模个体学习和遗忘动态

Result: 19.50%更快达到胜任时间；10.95%更高的终端掌握度；与专家教学判断95.45%一致率；将周转时间从11.58分钟缩短至34秒，减少95.08%

Conclusion: PACE作为协同驾驶系统可有效增强培训师决策能力，在实证研究和专家评估中均表现出色，有望缓解劳动力短缺带来的培训压力，实现大规模个性化教学

Abstract: 9-1-1 call-taking training requires mastery of over a thousand interdependent skills, covering diverse incident types and protocol-specific nuances. A nationwide labor shortage is already straining training capacity, but effective instruction still demands that trainers tailor objectives to each trainee's evolving competencies. This personalization burden is one that current practice cannot scale. Partnering with Metro Nashville Department of Emergency Communications (MNDEC), we propose PACE (Personalized Adaptive Curriculum Engine), a co-pilot system that augments trainer decision-making by (1) maintaining probabilistic beliefs over trainee skill states, (2) modeling individual learning and forgetting dynamics, and (3) recommending training scenarios that balance acquisition of new competencies with retention of existing ones. PACE propagates evidence over a structured skill graph to accelerate diagnostic coverage and applies contextual bandits to select scenarios that target gaps the trainee is prepared to address. Empirical results show that PACE achieves 19.50% faster time-to-competence and 10.95% higher terminal mastery compared to state-of-the-art frameworks. Co-pilot studies with practicing training officers further demonstrate a 95.45% alignment rate between PACE's and experts' pedagogical judgments on real-world cases. Under estimation, PACE cuts turnaround time to merely 34 seconds from 11.58 minutes, up to 95.08% reduction.

</details>


### [44] [Legal interpretation and AI: from expert systems to argumentation and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05392)
*Václav Janeček,Giovanni Sartor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文概述了人工智能与法律研究领域中法律解释的三种主要研究进路：专家系统、知识工程、论证理论以及机器学习，并探讨了这些方法在法律实践中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能与法律研究需要应对法律解释这一核心问题，随着技术发展，不同的方法论和研究范式不断涌现，需要梳理和理解这些方法的各自特点与贡献。

Method: 文章采用综述性分析方法，识别并分类了法律解释在AI与法律研究中的三种主要研究进路：专家系统研究（聚焦法律知识工程）、论证研究（关注解释性论证的结构与辩证交互）、机器学习研究（通过语言模型自动生成解释性建议）。

Result: 研究表明：专家系统方法致力于将人类生成的解释精确转移至知识库以实现一致应用；论证方法旨在表示解释性论证的结构及其辩证互动，以评估论证框架内解释性主张的可接受性；机器学习方法则专注于通过通用和专用语言模型自动生成解释性建议，当前已在法律实践中得到越来越广泛的应用。

Conclusion: AI与法律研究在法律解释方面形成了多元化的研究格局，三种方法各有侧重：专家系统强调知识表示的一致性，论证方法强调推理结构的严谨性，机器学习强调大规模文本的自动处理能力。这些方法共同推动了法律人工智能从符号主义向连接主义的演进。

Abstract: AI and Law research has encountered legal interpretation in different ways, in the context of its evolving approaches and methodologies. Research on expert system has focused on legal knowledge engineering, with the goal of ensuring that human-generated interpretations can be precisely transferred into knowledge-bases, to be consistently applied. Research on argumentation has aimed at representing the structure of interpretive arguments, as well as their dialectical interactions, to assess of the acceptability of interpretive claims within argumentation frameworks. Research on machine learning has focused on the automated generation of interpretive suggestions and arguments, through general and specialised language models, now being increasingly deployed in legal practice.

</details>

Abstract: Introspection is a foundational cognitive ability, but its mechanism is not well understood. Recent work has shown that AI models can introspect. We study their mechanism of introspection, first extensively replicating Lindsey et al. (2025)'s thought injection detection paradigm in large open-source models. We show that these models detect injected representations via two separable mechanisms: (i) probability-matching (inferring from perceived anomaly of the prompt) and (ii) direct access to internal states. The direct access mechanism is content-agnostic: models detect that an anomaly occurred but cannot reliably identify its semantic content. The two model classes we study confabulate injected concepts that are high-frequency and concrete (e.g., "apple'"); for them correct concept guesses typically require significantly more tokens. This content-agnostic introspective mechanism is consistent with leading theories in philosophy and psychology.

</details>


### [46] [Distributed Partial Information Puzzles: Examining Common Ground Construction Under Epistemic Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05450)
*Yifan Zhu,Mariah Bradford,Kenneth Lai,Timothy Obiso,Videep Venkatesha,James Pustejovsky,Nikhil Krishnaswamy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出分布式部分信息谜题(DPIP)作为协作建构任务，用于研究多模态多方协作中的共同 ground建立问题。构建了跨语音、手势和动作模态标注和对齐的多模态数据集，并评估了LLM和动态认知逻辑(DEL)两种范式。结果表明DPIP对LLM构成显著挑战，揭示了当前多模态AI系统在信念追踪方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立共同 ground(共享信念和相互认可的事实)是协作的基础，但在多模态多方设置中仍是AI系统的挑战，因为协作者带来不同的信息。当前AI系统尤其在信息不对称的多模态协作场景中难以有效建立和维护共同 ground。

Method: 提出两种范式：(1) 使用提示工程让LLM从多模态更新中推断共享信念；(2) 基于动态认知逻辑(DEL)的公理化流水线方法， incrementally执行相同任务。对两种方法在DPIP标注数据上进行评估比较。

Result: 实验结果表明DPIP对现代LLM追踪任务进度和信念状态的能力构成挑战，LLM在该任务上表现不佳，而DEL方法在该场景下显示出理论优势。

Conclusion: DPIP数据集对现代LLM在追踪任务进度和信念状态方面构成显著挑战，表明当前多模态大语言模型在建立共同 ground和信念追踪方面存在明显局限，DEL方法在该任务上显示出一定的理论优势。

Abstract: Establishing common ground, a shared set of beliefs and mutually recognized facts, is fundamental to collaboration, yet remains a challenge for current AI systems, especially in multimodal, multiparty settings, where the collaborators bring different information to the table. We introduce the Distributed Partial Information Puzzle (DPIP), a collaborative construction task that elicits rich multimodal communication under epistemic asymmetry. We present a multimodal dataset of these interactions, annotated and temporally aligned across speech, gesture, and action modalities to support reasoning over propositional content and belief dynamics. We then evaluate two paradigms for modeling common ground (CG): (1) state-of-the-art large language models (LLMs), prompted to infer shared beliefs from multimodal updates, and (2) an axiomatic pipeline grounded in Dynamic Epistemic Logic (DEL) that incrementally performs the same task. Results on the annotated DPIP data indicate that it poses a challenge to modern LLMs' abilities to track both task progression and belief state.

</details>


### [47] [Towards Provably Unbiased LLM Judges via Bias-Bounded Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05485)
*Benjamin Feuer,Lucas Rosenblatt,Oussama Elachqar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出A-BB框架，通过形式化方法约束LLM评判者的偏见，在Arena-Hard-Auto上验证可减少偏见的危害，同时保持61-99%的原始排名相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI模型向自主、自我维护的反馈循环发展，需要自动化、可验证的奖励和反馈来源。当真实标签稀少或非确定性时，LLM-as-a-Judge是实用的奖励来源。但现有LLM评判者缺乏强保证，尤其是当偏见向量未知或被对手发现时。

Method: 提出平均有界偏见(A-BB)算法框架，通过形式化方法保证减少LLM评判者中任何可测量偏见导致的伤害/影响。在Arena-Hard-Auto上对四个LLM评判者进行评估。

Result: 在格式化偏见和 schematic 偏见设置下，实现(tau=0.5, delta=0.01)的有界偏见保证，同时保持与原始排名61-99%的相关性，大多数评判者-偏见组合超过80%。

Conclusion: A-BB框架能够在保持与原始排名高度相关性的同时，有效约束LLM评判者的偏见，提供减少偏 见危害的正式保证。

Abstract: As AI models progress beyond simple chatbots into more complex workflows, we draw ever closer to the event horizon beyond which AI systems will be utilized in autonomous, self-maintaining feedback loops. Any autonomous AI system will depend on automated, verifiable rewards and feedback; in settings where ground truth is sparse or non-deterministic, one practical source of such rewards is an LLM-as-a-Judge. Although LLM judges continue to improve, the literature has yet to introduce systems capable of enforcing standards with strong guarantees, particularly when bias vectors are unknown or adversarially discovered. To remedy this issue, we propose average bias-boundedness (A-BB), an algorithmic framework which formally guarantees reductions of harm/impact as a result of any measurable bias in an LLM judge. Evaluating on Arena-Hard-Auto with four LLM judges, we achieve (tau=0.5, delta=0.01) bias-bounded guarantees while retaining 61-99% correlation with original rankings across formatting and schematic bias settings, with most judge-bias combinations exceeding 80%. The code to reproduce our findings is available at https://github.com/penfever/bias-bounded-evaluation.

</details>


### [48] [The Spike, the Sparse and the Sink: Anatomy of Massive Activations and Attention Sinks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05498)
*Shangwen Sun,Alfredo Canziani,Yann LeCun,Jiachen Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了Transformer语言模型中的两种现象：大规模激活（massive activations）和注意力锚点（attention sinks）。作者通过系统实验表明这两种现象主要是现代Transformer架构设计的产物，且具有不同功能：大激活产生跨层的近恒定隐藏表示，作为隐式参数；注意力锚点调节注意力输出并偏向短程依赖。pre-norm配置是导致两者共现的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作观察到大规模激活和注意力锚点这两种现象经常同时出现，且涉及相同的token，但它们的功能角色和因果关系尚不清楚。

Method: 通过系统性实验，研究这两种现象的共现机制和功能差异。

Result: 发现两种现象的共现主要是现代Transformer设计的架构artifact。两者服务于相关但不同的功能：大规模激活全局运作，诱导跨层持续存在的近恒定隐藏表示；注意力锚点局部运作，调节跨头部的注意力输出。pre-norm配置是导致共现的关键设计选择。

Conclusion: pre-norm配置是使两种现象共现的关键因素，消除pre-norm会导致两种现象解耦。研究揭示了Transformer架构中这两个重要现象的本质区别和各自的功能角色。

Abstract: We study two recurring phenomena in Transformer language models: massive activations, in which a small number of tokens exhibit extreme outliers in a few channels, and attention sinks, in which certain tokens attract disproportionate attention mass regardless of semantic relevance. Prior work observes that these phenomena frequently co-occur and often involve the same tokens, but their functional roles and causal relationship remain unclear. Through systematic experiments, we show that the co-occurrence is largely an architectural artifact of modern Transformer design, and that the two phenomena serve related but distinct functions. Massive activations operate globally: they induce near-constant hidden representations that persist across layers, effectively functioning as implicit parameters of the model. Attention sinks operate locally: they modulate attention outputs across heads and bias individual heads toward short-range dependencies. We identify the pre-norm configuration as the key choice that enables the co-occurrence, and show that ablating it causes the two phenomena to decouple.

</details>

### [49] [Towards a B+-tree with Fluctuation-Free Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04785)
*Lu Xing,Walid G. Aref*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出FFBtree，一种消除B+树分裂传播的可预测插入算法，通过预分裂关键节点将单次插入的I/O成本波动从H+1到3H+1限制在H+1以内，实现波动-free性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代DBMS中索引维护可能触发罕见但严重的I/O尖峰，传统B+树中节点分裂传播导致单次插入成本从H+1到3H+1变化，近3倍性能退化。

Method: 1)形式化性能波动为最佳/最坏情况插入行为的差距；2)引入安全节点和关键节点概念捕获不可避免的分裂；3)FFBtree在插入时预分裂关键节点；4)证明从根到叶路径最多遇到一个必须分裂的关键节点；5)使用乐观锁耦合实现并发控制。

Result: 模拟索引实验表明FFBtree通过消除分裂传播限制I/O波动，与传统基线相比持续减少插入尖峰；真实索引实验确认了类似改进。

Conclusion: FFBtree成功实现了无分裂传播的插入操作，将I/O波动限制在常数范围内，为DBMS提供了可预测的索引性能。

Abstract: Performance predictability is critical for modern DBMSs because index maintenance can trigger rare but severe I/O spikes. In a B or B+-tree with height H, node split propagation means the cost of a single insert can vary from H + 1 to 3H + 1 I/Os when splits reach the root, nearly a three times degradation. We formalize performance fluctuation as the gap between best- and worst-case insert behavior and introduce the notions of safe and critical nodes to capture when splits become unavoidable. We introduce the FFBtree, a B+-tree insert algorithm that preemptively splits some critical nodes, and prove that when navigating from root to leaf the insert algorithm will encounter at most one critical node that must be split, ensuring no split propagation can occur and producing fluctuation-free performance. Our implementation maintains critical-node metadata efficiently and integrates with optimistic lock coupling for concurrency. Experiments with simulated indexes show the FFBtree caps I/O fluctuation by eliminating split propagation and consistently reduces insert spikes compared to conventional baselines, and real-index experiments confirm comparable improvements.

</details>


### [50] [Beyond Linear LLM Invocation: An Efficient and Effective Semantic Filter Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04799)
*Nan Hou,Kangfei Zhao,Jiadong Xie,Jeffrey Xu Yu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出CSV框架，通过将元组嵌入语义聚类、采样小子集进行LLM评估，并使用UniVote和SimVote两种投票策略推断聚类级标签，将LLM调用次数降低至亚线性复杂度，同时保持可比的准确率和F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义过滤器操作符需要对表进行逐元组评估，导致完整的线性扫描，造成高昂的延迟和token成本。现有优化方法仍无法突破线性LLM调用壁垒。

Method: 提出聚类-采样-投票(CSV)框架：1)将元组嵌入语义聚类；2)采样小 subset 进行LLM评估；3)通过两种投票策略推断聚类级标签——UniVote（统一投票）和SimVote（按语义相似度加权投票）；4)对模糊聚类触发重聚类以确保鲁棒性。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验表明，CSV相比最先进方法将LLM调用次数降低1.28-355倍，同时保持相当的准确率和F1分数。

Conclusion: CSV成功将LLM调用复杂度从线性降低到亚线性，为大规模语义查询处理提供了一种有效的优化方案，具有实际的工程应用价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for semantic query processing over large corpora. A set of semantic operators derived from relational algebra has been proposed to provide a unified interface for expressing such queries, among which the semantic filter operator serves as a cornerstone. Given a table T with a natural language predicate e, for each tuple in the relation, the execution of a semantic filter proceeds by constructing an input prompt that combines the predicate e with its content, querying the LLM, and obtaining the binary decision. However, this tuple-by-tuple evaluation necessitates a complete linear scan of the table, incurring prohibitive latency and token costs. Although recent work has attempted to optimize semantic filtering, it still does not break the linear LLM invocation barriers. To address this, we propose Clustering-Sampling-Voting (CSV), a new framework that reduces LLM invocations to sublinear complexity while providing error guarantees. CSV embeds tuples into semantic clusters, samples a small subset for LLM evaluation, and infers cluster-level labels via two proposed voting strategies: UniVote, which aggregates labels uniformly, and SimVote, which weights votes by semantic similarity. Moreover, CSV triggers re-clustering on ambiguous clusters to ensure robustness across diverse datasets. The results conducted on real-world datasets demonstrate that CSV reduces the number of LLM calls by 1.28-355x compared to the state-of-the-art approaches, while maintaining comparable effectiveness in terms of Accuracy and F1 score.

</details>


### [51] [Deterministic Preprocessing and Interpretable Fuzzy Banding for Cost-per-Student Reporting from Extracted Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04905)
*Shane Lee,Stella Ng*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 一种确定性文件工作流处理学术数据库导出的电子表格，聚合成本和学生数据计算成本-学生比，通过SHA-256哈希确保可重现性，并添加模糊波段分类支持年度内决策解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行政提取物常作为电子表格交换，在预算、工作量审查和治理讨论中被直接读取为报告。当导出的工作簿成为决策的参考快照时，需要通过重新计算来验证转换的正确性。

Method: 在cad_processor.py中实现确定性、规则驱动的文件工作流：1)摄入CAD导出工作簿并聚合学科-年度和学院-年度的包容性成本和学生数；2)计算成本-学生比；3)生成包含处理摘要、趋势分析、报告和模糊波段四个工作表的输出工作簿；4)使用左肩、三角和右肩函数计算模糊波段成员权重，采用固定优先级(中、低、高)进行确定性决胜。

Result: 生成了四种输出工作表：处理摘要(运行记录和计数器)、趋势分析(学院年度成本-学生比矩阵)、报告(宽学科级表)、模糊波段(年度锚点、成员权重和波段标签)。模糊波段将有限正成本-学生比值标记为低、中、高，作为决策支持信号而非概率。

Conclusion: 实现了可重现的计算工作流，通过SHA-256哈希确保输入快照的精确匹配重新计算，支持预算、工作量审查和治理决策的验证。

Abstract: Administrative extracts are often exchanged as spreadsheets and may be read as reports in their own right during budgeting, workload review, and governance discussions. When an exported workbook becomes the reference snapshot for such decisions, the transformation can be checked by recomputation against a clearly identified input.
  A deterministic, rule-governed, file-based workflow is implemented in cad_processor.py. The script ingests a Casual Academic Database (CAD) export workbook and aggregates inclusive on-costs and student counts into subject-year and school-year totals, from which it derives cost-per-student ratios. It writes a processed workbook with four sheets: Processing Summary (run record and counters), Trend Analysis (schoolyear cost-per-student matrix), Report (wide subject-level table), and Fuzzy Bands (per-year anchors, membership weights, and band labels). The run record includes a SHA-256 hash of the input workbook bytes to support snapshot-matched recomputation.
  For within-year interpretation, the workflow adds a simple fuzzy banding layer that labels finite, positive school-year cost-per-student values as Low, Medium, or High. The per-year anchors are the minimum, median, and maximum of the finite, positive ratios. Membership weights are computed using left-shoulder, triangular, and right-shoulder functions, with deterministic tie-breaking in a fixed priority order (Medium, then Low, then High). These weights are treated as decision-support signals rather than probabilities.
  A worked example provides a reproducible calculation of a band assignment from the reported anchors and ratios. Supplementary material includes a claim-to-evidence matrix, a reproducibility note, and a short glossary that links selected statements to code and workbook artefacts.

</details>


### [52] [FluxSieve: Unifying Streaming and Analytical Data Planes for Scalable Cloud Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04937)
*Adriano Vogel,Sören Henning,Otmar Ertl*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: FluxSieve通过在数据摄取路径中嵌入流内预计算和过滤层，统一流处理与分析查询，实现查询性能数量级提升且开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据平台在处理高并发和计算密集型查询时面临困难，特别是大规模可观测性平台处理高容量、高速度数据记录时。昂贵的过滤查询在查询时产生大量计算和存储开销。

Method: 提出FluxSieve统一架构，将拉取式查询处理与推送式流处理相结合，在数据摄取路径中嵌入轻量级流内预计算和过滤层。包含三个核心设计：(1)统一流和分析数据平面的基础架构，(2)可扩展多模式匹配机制支持并发评估和即时更新过滤规则，(3)集成Apache Pinot和DuckDB两种开源分析系统。

Result: 在多个系统、查询类型和性能指标上评估显示，查询性能提升高达数量级，额外存储成本可忽略不计，计算开销非常低。

Conclusion: FluxSieve成功通过在数据摄取时进行预处理和过滤，避免了运行专用流处理框架的复杂性和运维负担，实现了查询性能的数量级提升。

Abstract: Despite many advances in query optimization, indexing techniques, and data storage, modern data platforms still face difficulties in delivering robust query performance under high concurrency and computationally intensive queries. This challenge is particularly pronounced in large-scale observability platforms handling high-volume, high-velocity data records. For instance, recurrent, expensive filtering queries at query time impose substantial computational and storage overheads in the analytical data plane. In this paper, we propose FluxSieve, a unified architecture that reconciles traditional pull-based query processing with push-based stream processing by embedding a lightweight in-stream precomputation and filtering layer directly into the data ingestion path. This avoids the complexity and operational burden of running queries in dedicated stream processing frameworks. Concretely, this work (i) introduces a foundational architecture that unifies streaming and analytical data planes via in-stream filtering and records enrichment, (ii) designs a scalable multi-pattern matching mechanism that supports concurrent evaluation and on-the-fly updates of filtering rules with minimal per-record overhead, (iii) demonstrates how to integrate this ingestion-time processing with two open-source analytical systems -- Apache Pinot as a Real-Time Online Analytical Processing (RTOLAP) engine and DuckDB as an embedded analytical database, and (iv) performs comprehensive experimental evaluation of our approach. Our evaluation across different systems, query types, and performance metrics shows up to orders-of-magnitude improvements in query performance at the cost of negligible additional storage and very low computational overhead.

</details>


### [53] [CRISP: Correlation-Resilient Indexing via Subspace Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05180)
*Dimitris Dimitropoulos,Achilleas Michalopoulos,Dimitrios Tsitsigkos,Nikos Mamoulis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: CRISP是一个用于高维空间ANN搜索的框架，通过自适应方差重分布策略和CSR索引结构解决现有方法在高维场景下的内存和预处理开销问题，并提供双模式查询引擎平衡召回率和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代学习表示的维度增加到数千维后，现有的SOTA ANN索引面临严重局限：图方法(HNSW)存在内存消耗过大和路由质量下降问题；量化/旋转方法(RaBitQ, OPQ)预处理开销过高。现有刚性流水线对所有数据无差别应用正交旋转，缺乏针对性优化。

Method: 提出三个核心创新：(1)轻量级相关性感知自适应策略，只在必要时重分布方差，降低预处理复杂度；(2)缓存友好的CSR压缩稀疏行索引结构；(3)多阶段双模式查询引擎——Guaranteed Mode保留理论召回率下界，Optimized Mode利用基于排名的加权评分和早停机制降低延迟。

Result: 在高达4096维的数据集上广泛评估表明，CRISP实现了SOTA查询吞吐量、低构建成本和峰值内存效率。

Conclusion: CRISP通过自适应机制和高效索引结构成功解决了高维ANN搜索中的内存、预处理延迟和查询效率问题，为非常高维空间的近似最近邻搜索提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: As the dimensionality of modern learned representations increases to thousands of dimensions, the state-of-the-art Approximate Nearest Neighbor (ANN) indices exhibit severe limitations. Graph-based methods (e.g., HNSW) suffer from prohibitive memory consumption and routing degradation, while recent randomized quantization and learned rotation approaches (e.g., RaBitQ, OPQ) impose significant preprocessing overheads. We introduce CRISP, a novel framework designed for ANN search in very-high-dimensional spaces. Unlike rigid pipelines that apply expensive orthogonal rotations indiscriminately, CRISP employs a lightweight, correlation- aware adaptive strategy that redistributes variance only when necessary, effectively reducing the preprocessing complexity. We couple this adaptive mechanism with a cache-coherent Compressed Sparse Row (CSR) index structure. Furthermore, CRISP incorporates a multi-stage dual-mode query engine: a Guaranteed Mode that preserves rigorous theoretical lower bounds on recall, and an Optimized Mode that leverages rank-based weighted scoring and early termination to reduce query latency. Extensive evaluation on datasets of very high dimensionality (up to 4096) demonstrates that CRISP achieves state-of-the-art query throughput, low construction costs, and peak memory efficiency.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

### [54] [iScript: A Domain-Adapted Large Language Model and Benchmark for Physical Design Tcl Script Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04476)
*Ning Xu,Zhaoyang Zhang,Senlin Shu,Lei Qi,Jiaqi Lv,Wensuo Wang,Tianhao Zhao,Chao Zhang,Zhaoliang Yang,Xiangyu Li,Zhaorui Su,Jingshan Li,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了iScript，一个针对Innovus Tcl脚本生成领域适配的Qwen3-8B模型，以及iScript-Bench基准测试，旨在解决现代EDA流程中Tcl脚本生成的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代EDA流程严重依赖Tcl脚本，但通用LLM在该领域表现不佳，原因包括：极端的数据稀缺、领域特定的语义以及物理设计所需的高可靠性。

Method: 1. 多阶段数据合成管道：整合命令提取、静态linting、需求回溯推理和思维链生成，产生10K元组（需求、CoT、脚本）数据集；2. 两阶段训练策略：结合领域自适应预训练和监督微调；3. 两步验证框架：静态语法验证和基于LLM的功能评估。

Result: iScript在基准测试中平均pass@k得分高于当前最先进的LLM。

Conclusion: 领域适配和数据合成方法对EDA脚本生成任务有效。

Abstract: Modern EDA flows rely heavily on Tcl scripting, yet general LLMs perform poorly in this domain due to extreme data scarcity, domain-specific semantics, and the high reliability required in physical design. We present iScript, a domain-adapted Qwen3-8B model for Innovus Tcl script generation, and iScript-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark covering five task categories and three difficulty levels. To overcome the lack of training data, we introduce a multi-stage data synthesis pipeline that integrates command extraction, static linting, requirement back-inference, and Chain-of-Thought generation, producing a 10K-tuple (requirement, CoT, script) dataset. iScript is trained through a two-stage strategy combining domain-adaptive pretraining and supervised fine-tuning. To evaluate script correctness efficiently, we further propose a two-step verification framework consisting of static syntax verification and LLM-based functional evaluation. On our benchmark, iScript shows higher pass@k scores than currently state-of-the-art LLMs on average. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of domain adaptation and data synthesis for EDA scripting tasks.

</details>


### [55] [CLARC: C/C++ Benchmark for Robust Code Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04484)
*Kaicheng Wang,Liyan Huang,Weike Fang,Weihang Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了CLARC，一个用于C/C++的代码检索基准测试，包含1,245个评估对和5,472个训练对。通过自动化流水线确保代码可编译性并按依赖复杂度分类。实验表明6个SOTA模型在匿名化和编译到低级语言时性能大幅下降，揭示了模型对词法特征的依赖而非语义理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码检索基准测试主要集中在Python且缺乏对鲁棒性的深度测试，仅停留在表面词法层面。缺乏针对C/C++的真实代码检索基准。

Method: 构建了自动化的代码搜索数据集流水线，确保代码可编译性，按依赖复杂度（自定义类型和辅助函数）对代码片段分类。引入挑战性设置测试检索鲁棒性，包括标识符匿名化以及编译到汇编和WebAssembly。对6个最先进的代码检索模型进行了评估。

Result: 6个最先进的代码检索模型在挑战性条件下表现出明显的性能下降，证实了模型持续依赖词法特征而非代码语义理解。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前最先进代码检索模型在语义理解方面的根本性缺陷，其性能在匿名化和编译到低级语言等挑战性设置下显著下降。模型过度依赖词法特征而非真正的代码语义理解，需要改进。数据集已公开可用于进一步研究。

Abstract: Efficient code retrieval is critical for developer productivity, yet existing benchmarks largely focus on Python and rarely stress-test robustness beyond superficial lexical cues. To address the gap, we introduce an automated pipeline for code search datasets and present CLARC, a C/C++ benchmark built from real-world GitHub repositories. CLARC contains 1,245 query-code pairs for evaluation and 5,472 pairs for training. The benchmark incorporates LLM-generated natural language queries validated through rigorous human scoring and hypothesis testing. To analyze contextual requirements effectively, our pipeline starts by ensuring code compilability. It then categorizes code snippets by dependency complexity, distinguishing whether the code relies on custom-defined types or helper functions. The pipeline also enables CLARC to stress-test retrieval robustness by introducing challenging settings, including identifier anonymization and compilation to low-level languages like Assembly and WebAssembly. Under these conditions, our evaluation of six state-of-the-art models reveals sharp drops in retrieval effectiveness. The experimental results highlight the models' persistent reliance on lexical features rather than code semantic understanding. Our dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ClarcTeam/CLARC.

</details>


### [56] [Industrial Survey on Robustness Testing In Cyber Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04587)
*Christophe Ponsard,Abiola Paterne Chokki,Jean-François Daune*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过在瓦隆尼亚地区开展的工业调查，评估CPS健壮性的当前实践状态，研究其与需求工程、系统设计、测试执行、故障模式和可用工具的关系，识别行业实践与最新方法论之间的关键差距，并与文献中的类似工业调查进行对比


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS在现代工业中扮演关键角色，其故障可能导致严重的经济、运营和安全后果，因此确保系统健壮性至关重要

Method: 在瓦隆尼亚地区开展的工业调查，涵盖多个行业领域

Result: 识别出CPS健壮性面临的关键挑战和差距，包括需求工程、系统设计、测试执行、故障模式及工具支持等方面的实践与先进方法论之间的不一致

Conclusion: 研究揭示了工业实践与最新方法论之间存在显著差距，为未来CPS健壮性研究提供了方向

Abstract: Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) play a critical role in modern industrial domains, including manufacturing, energy, transportation, and healthcare, where they enable automation, optimization, and real-time decision-making. Ensuring the robustness of these systems is paramount, as failures can have significant economic, operational, and safety consequences. This paper present findings from an industrial survey conducted in Wallonia, covering a wide range of sectors, to assess the current state of practice in CPS robustness. It investigates robustness from how it is understood and applied in relationship with requirements engineering, system design, test execution, failure modes, and available tools. It identifies key challenges and gaps between industry practices and state-of-the-art methodologies. Additionally, it compares our findings with similar industrial surveys from the literature.

</details>


### [57] [Behaviour Driven Development Scenario Generation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04729)
*Amila Rathnayake,Mojtaba Shahin,Golnoush Abaei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了GPT-4、Claude 3和Gemini三种大型语言模型在自动化行为驱动开发(BDD)场景生成任务中的表现，使用了包含500个用户故事、需求描述及其对应BDD场景的数据集进行多维评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为驱动开发(BDD)场景的手动编写耗时且容易出错，自动化生成BDD场景可以提高软件开发和测试效率。然而，不同LLM在生成BDD场景方面的能力差异尚不明确，需要系统性地评估和比较。

Method: 构建包含500个用户故事、需求描述及对应BDD场景的数据集(来自四个专有软件产品)；采用多维评估框架，包括文本相似度、语义相似度指标、基于LLM的评估和人类专家评估；测试了不同的提示技术(零样本、思维链、少样本)和采样参数(温度、top_p)。

Result: GPT-4在文本和语义相似度指标上得分最高，但Claude 3生成的场景在人类专家和LLM评估器中评分最高；DeepSeek作为LLM评估器与人类判断的相关性强于文本/语义相似度指标；提示技术效果具有模型特异性(GPT-4零样本最优，Claude 3思维链最优，Gemini少样本最优)；输入质量决定生成效果(详细需求描述生成高质量场景，用户故事单独使用时生成质量低)；温度0和top_p 1.0在所有模型上产生最高质量的BDD场景。

Conclusion: 不同LLM在不同评估维度上各有优势，选择LLM时应考虑具体评估标准；提示工程策略需针对特定模型进行优化；输入质量是BDD场景生成的关键因素；基于LLM的评估器(如DeepSeek)可能比传统相似度指标更适合评估生成质量。

Abstract: This paper presents an evaluation of three LLMs, GPT-4, Claude 3, and Gemini, for automated Behaviour-Driven Development (BDD) scenarios generation. To support this evaluation, we constructed a dataset of 500 user stories, requirement descriptions, and their corresponding BDD scenarios, drawn from four proprietary software products. We assessed the quality of BDD scenarios generated by LLMs using a multidimensional evaluation framework encompassing text and semantic similarity metrics, LLM-based evaluation, and human expert assessment. Our findings reveal that although GPT-4 achieves higher scores in text and semantic similarity metrics, Claude 3 produces scenarios rated highest by both human experts and LLM-based evaluators. LLM-based evaluators, particularly DeepSeek, show a stronger correlation with human judgment than with text similarity and semantic similarity metrics. The effectiveness of prompting techniques is model-specific: GPT-4 performs best with zero-shot, Claude 3 benefits from chain-of-thought reasoning, and Gemini achieves optimal results with few-shot examples. Input quality determines the effectiveness of BDD scenario generation: detailed requirement descriptions alone yield high-quality scenarios, whereas user stories alone yield low-quality scenarios. Our experiments indicate that setting temperature to 0 and top_p to 1.0 produced the highest-quality BDD scenarios across all models.

</details>


### [58] [Public Sector Open Source Program Offices - Archetypes for how to Grow (Common) Institutional Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04891)
*Johan Linåker,Astor Nummelin Carlberg,Ciaran O'Riordan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了欧盟、挪威、列支敦士登和冰岛的公共部门组织中开源项目办公室（OSPO）如何促进开源软件的采用、开发和协作。通过对16个案例的访谈调查，研究识别出六种OSPO原型，并提出政策建议，为公共部门设计适合自身情境的OSPO提供实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源软件是支撑工业和公共数字服务的基础设施的关键组成部分，在欧洲公共部门的重要性通过多项部长级声明得到强调，强调其加速数字化转型、培养数字技能人口的潜力。然而，如何通过组织支持功能或能力中心（即OSPO）来促进OSS的采用、开发和协作，目前尚缺乏系统性的研究。

Method: 采用定性研究方法，对16个公共部门OSPO案例中的18名OSPO代表进行访谈调查，并将这些案例交叉分析和归纳为六种OSPO原型。通过两个后续焦点小组验证和丰富研究结果，参与者包括之前的受访者和额外专家。

Result: 识别出六种不同的OSPO原型，揭示了它们的组织结构、职责和对OSS采用的贡献。这六种原型连同政策建议，为PSO如何根据其特定背景、资源和政策目标设计自己的OSPO提供了指导。

Conclusion: 研究结果增强了对OSPO作为促进OSS采用的整体战略举措的理解，为PSO和决策者提供了关于利用OSS实现战略目标、促进数字主权、推动经济增长以及提高数字服务的互操作性和质量的实践指导。

Abstract: Context: Open Source Software (OSS) is a crucial component of over 90\% of digital infrastructure underpinning industry and public digital services, facilitating collaborative software development and dissemination. Its significance in the European public sector has been emphasised through various Ministerial Declarations, highlighting its potential to accelerate digitalisation, transform businesses, and foster a digitally skilled population. Research Aim: This study aims to explore how the adoption, development, and collaboration on OSS can be enabled through organisational support functions or centres of competency, also known as Open Source Programme Offices (OSPOs) within Public Sector Organisations (PSOs) in the European Union, Norway, Liechtenstein, and Iceland. Methodology: A qualitative research approach was adopted, involving an interview survey of 18 OSPO representatives across 16 cases of public-sector OSPOs. These cases were cross-analysed and categorised into six OSPO archetypes. The findings were validated and enriched through two follow-up focus groups that included earlier interviewees and additional experts. Results: The study identified six distinct OSPO archetypes, providing insights into their organisational structures, responsibilities, and contributions to OSS adoption. The archetypes, along with policy recommendations, offer guidance on how PSOs can design their own OSPOs, taking into account their specific context, resources, and policy goals. Conclusions: The findings enhance the understanding of OSPOs as strategic endeavours aimed at promoting OSS adoption. The study offers practical guidance for PSOs and policymakers on leveraging OSS to achieve strategic objectives, foster digital sovereignty, drive economic growth, and improve the interoperability and quality of digital services.

</details>


### [59] [Why Do You Contribute to Stack Overflow? Understanding Cross-Cultural Motivations and Usage Patterns before the Age of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05043)
*Sherlock A. Licorish,Elijah Zolduoarrati,Tony Savarimuthu,Rashina Hoda,Ronnie De Souza Santos,Pankajeshwara Sharma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究调查了Stack Overflow贡献者的动机，分析了美国、中国和俄罗斯之间的区域差异及其与平台活动的关系。通过定性内容分析和定量语言分析，识别出17种动机类别，发现广告机会和利他性问题解决是主要动机，美国贡献者表现出更强的自我推广行为，中国贡献者表现出更强的学习导向参与。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM时代，理解社区问答平台贡献者的参与动机对维持知识共享生态系统至关重要，但目前对不同国家文化背景下贡献者动机的差异了解有限。

Method: 结合Stack Overflow档案的定性内容分析与美国、中国和俄罗斯数据的定量语言分析，使用演绎内容分析识别动机类别，并应用相关性分析识别动机与平台活动之间的关联。

Result: 识别出17种动机类别；主要动机为广告机会和利他性问题解决；美国贡献者自我推广行为更强；中国贡献者学习导向参与度更高；详细档案用户更倾向于广告和社交活动，学习导向用户自我呈现较少。

Conclusion: 不同文化背景下贡献者动机存在显著差异，理解这些差异可为提升软件工程领域跨文化参与度的策略提供参考。

Abstract: Understanding motivations of contributors for participating in community question and answer platforms is crucial for sustaining knowledge-sharing ecosystem, which is necessary to advance the discipline while also ensuring its longevity. This is particularly necessary in the age of LLMs, where data from such portals are used to train these models. Limited insights exist regarding how motivations of contributors vary across different national cultures. This research investigates Stack Overflow contributor motivations, analysing regional differences and relations to platform activity. We combined qualitative content analysis of Stack Overflow profiles with quantitative linguistic analysis of data from the United States, China, and Russia. Using deductive content analysis, we identified 17 motivational categories. We applied correlation analysis to identify associations between stated motivations and platform activities. Contributors are primarily motivated by advertising opportunities and altruistic problem solving desires. American contributors demonstrated stronger self promotional behaviours while Chinese contributors exhibited greater learning oriented engagement. Our correlation analysis showed that those with more detailed profiles tend to engage in advertising and social activities, while learning oriented users maintain minimal self presentation. Understanding these variations can inform strategies for enhancing cross cultural participation in software engineering.

</details>


### [60] [A Benchmarking Framework for Model Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05250)
*Philipp-Lorenz Glaser,Lola Burgueño,Dominik Bork*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对模型驱动工程(MDE)中软件模型数据集的基准测试框架，旨在系统化评估数据集的质量、代表性和适用性，以解决当前数据集创建和使用中存在的质量不可比、代表性和可重复性不足等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前模型驱动工程中的实证研究和基于大语言模型的研究越来越多地依赖软件模型数据集，但这些数据集通常是临时创建的，缺乏针对特定任务的质量保证，导致研究结果难以比较、数据集质量和代表性不透明、可重复性差且可能存在偏差。

Method: 提出了一个MDE基准测试平台(Benchmark Platform for MDE)，提供统一的基础设施，使用定义的标准和指标，系统化地评估和比较不同语言和格式的软件模型数据集。

Result: 该框架能够对数据集进行系统性质量评估、代表性测量和特定任务适用性判断，为数据集作为第一类工件的处理和评估提供了方法论支持。

Conclusion: 通过建立标准化的数据集基准测试流程，有望提高MDE领域研究的可重复性、结果可比性，并减少数据集偏差，为机器学习技术在建模支持中的应用提供更高质量的数据基础。

Abstract: Empirical and LLM-based research in model-driven engineering increasingly relies on datasets of software models, for instance, to train or evaluate machine learning techniques for modeling support. These datasets have a significant impact on solution performance; hence, they should be treated and assessed as first-class artifacts. However, such datasets are typically collected or created ad hoc and without guarantees of their quality for the specific task for which they are used. This limits the comparability of results between studies, obscures dataset quality and representativeness, and leads to weak reproducibility and potential bias. In this work, we propose a benchmarking framework for model datasets (i.e., benchmarking the dataset itself). Benchmarking datasets involves systematically measuring their quality, representativeness, and suitability for specific tasks. To this end, we propose a Benchmark Platform for MDE that provides a unified infrastructure for systematically assessing and comparing datasets of software models across languages and formats, using defined criteria and metrics.

</details>


### [61] [A framework for assessing the capabilities of code generation of constraint domain-specific languages with large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05278)
*David Delgado,Lola Burgueño,Robert Clarisó*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个通用框架，用于评估大语言模型(LLM)从文本规范生成领域特定语言(DSL)代码的能力，并以约束语言(OCL和Alloy)为实例，与Python进行对比实验。研究发现LLM对Python的表现优于DSL，小上下文窗口的开源LLM无法生成约束相关代码，代码修复和多尝试策略可提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在支持软件开发任务(如代码补全和代码生成)方面表现出色，但当涉及不太流行的编程语言(如DSL)时，其有效性显著下降。DSL在特定领域建模中很重要，但缺乏针对LLM生成能力的系统评估。

Method: 提出一个通用的DSL代码生成评估框架，从形式正确性(well-formedness)和功能正确性(correctness)两个角度评估生成的代码。实验专注于约束语言(OCL和Alloy)，并与通用编程语言Python对比。评估了不同策略的影响，包括代码修复(code repair)、多尝试(multiple attempts)和提示模板选择。

Result: 实验结果表明：1)LLM对Python的表现总体优于OCL和Alloy；2)小上下文窗口的开源LLM无法生成约束相关代码，因为需要同时管理约束和领域模型；3)代码修复和多尝试策略可以提升生成代码质量；4)提示模板选择对结果影响较小。

Conclusion: 该评估框架可以系统分析代码生成的各个维度，帮助确定针对特定任务类型设置代码生成的最佳方式。研究为DSL代码生成任务中LLM的选择和配置提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can be used to support software development tasks, e.g., through code completion or code generation. However, their effectiveness drops significantly when considering less popular programming languages such as domain-specific languages (DSLs). In this paper, we propose a generic framework for evaluating the capabilities of LLMs generating DSL code from textual specifications. The generated code is assessed from the perspectives of well-formedness and correctness. This framework is applied to a particular type of DSL, constraint languages, focusing our experiments on OCL and Alloy and comparing their results to those achieved for Python, a popular general-purpose programming language. Experimental results show that, in general, LLMs have better performance for Python than for OCL and Alloy. LLMs with smaller context windows such as open-source LLMs may be unable to generate constraint-related code, as this requires managing both the constraint and the domain model where it is defined. Moreover, some improvements to the code generation process such as code repair (asking an LLM to fix incorrect code) or multiple attempts (generating several candidates for each coding task) can improve the quality of the generated code. Meanwhile, other decisions like the choice of a prompt template have less impact. All these dimensions can be systematically analyzed using our evaluation framework, making it possible to decide the most effective way to set up code generation for a particular type of task.

</details>

### [62] [Overcoming Latency-bound Limitations of Distributed Graph Algorithms using the HPX Runtime System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04583)
*Karame Mohammadiporshokooh,Panagiotis Syskakis,Andrew Lumsdaine,Hartmut Kaiser*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出基于HPX的分布式图处理库原型，通过异步执行和细粒度并行性解决大规模图处理中的负载不平衡和同步开销问题，实现的BFS、PageRank和三角形计数算法性能优于GraphX和PBGL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模图处理面临图结构不规则、图算法固有的延迟绑定特性以及分布式执行开销等挑战。现有框架（如Spark GraphX和PBGL）存在负载不平衡和同步开销问题。

Method: 使用C++机制（NWgraph库）和HPX的分布式容器与异步构造实现分布式图算法库原型，开发了BFS、PageRank和三角形计数三种算法，分别代表遍历、中心性和模式匹配类别。

Result: HPX高性能运行时的实现通过利用异步执行、延迟隐藏和细粒度共享内存并行性，显著优于传统GraphX和PBGL框架。

Conclusion: 统一执行模型使用相同的编程抽象表达本地和远程计算，运行时透明管理异步性，在每个节点利用共享内存并行性并重叠计算和通信，为扩展到更广泛的分布式图算法提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Graph processing at scale presents many challenges, including the irregular structure of graphs, the latency-bound nature of graph algorithms, and the overhead associated with distributed execution. While existing frameworks such as Spark GraphX and the Parallel Boost Graph Library (PBGL) have introduced abstractions for distributed graph processing, they continue to struggle with inherent issues like load imbalance and synchronization overhead. In this work, we present a distributed library prototype and a distributed implementation of three key graph algorithms - Breadth-First Search (BFS), PageRank, and Triangle Counting, using C++ mechanisms from the NWgraph library and leveraging HPX's distributed containers and asynchronous constructs. These algorithms span the categories of Traversal, centrality, and Pattern matching, and are selected to represent diverse computational characteristics. We evaluate our HPX-based implementations against GraphX, and PBGL, showing that a high-performance runtime such as HPX enables the construction of algorithms that significantly outperform conventional frameworks by exploiting asynchronous execution, latency hiding, and fine-grained parallelism in shared memory. All algorithms in our prototype follow a unified execution model in which local and remote computations are expressed using the same programming abstractions, with asynchrony managed transparently by the runtime. This design explicitly leverages shared-memory parallelism within each locality while overlapping communication and computation across localities, providing a practical foundation for extending this approach to a broader class of distributed graph algorithms.

</details>


### [63] [DuaLip-GPU Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04621)
*Gregory Dexter,Aida Rahmattalabi,Sanjana Garg,Qinquan Song,Ruby Tu,Yuan Gao,Yi Zhang,Zhipeng Wang,Rahul Mazumder*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对大规模线性规划的重构求解器架构，通过解耦问题规范与优化引擎、采用算子中心编程模型、针对GPU执行优化（包括稀疏匹配约束的约束对齐稀疏布局、批量投影核及仅通信对偶变量的分布式设计），在极端规模匹配工作负载上实现了相比先前分布式CPU求解器至少10倍的加速，同时保持收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模线性规划广泛存在于排名、分配和匹配等决策系统中。先前工作（ECLIPSE、DuaLip）虽证明岭正则化对偶上升方法可扩展，但实现紧密耦合于少量模式，且基于CPU中心的Scala/Spark栈，限制可扩展性且无法有效利用现代GPU加速器。

Method: 1. 重构求解器架构：解耦问题规范与优化引擎，采用算子中心编程模型，通过可组合原语表达对偶目标评估及分块投影算子表达可分解约束族；2. GPU执行技术：针对稀疏匹配约束设计约束对齐稀疏布局、批量投影核及仅通信对偶变量的分布式设计；3. 改进岭正则化对偶上升方法：引入Jacobi风格行归一化、原尺度缩放及正则化参数延续策略。

Result: 在极端规模匹配工作负载上，GPU实现在匹配停止标准下相比先前分布式CPU DuaLip求解器实现至少10倍wall-clock加速，同时保持收敛保证。

Conclusion: 新架构实现了高效GPU加速的大规模线性规划求解，显著提升性能的同时维持理论收敛保证，为排序、分配及匹配等大规模决策问题提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Large-scale linear programs (LPs) arise in many decision systems, including ranking, allocation, and matching problems that must be solved repeatedly at massive scale. Prior work such as ECLIPSE and LinkedIn's open-source DuaLip showed that ridge-regularized dual ascent with first-order methods can scale to these settings. However, the original implementation was tightly coupled to a small number of schemas and built on a CPU-centric Scala/Spark stack, limiting extensibility and preventing effective use of modern accelerators.
  We present a redesigned solver architecture that decouples problem specification from the optimization engine and targets GPU execution. The system uses an operator-centric programming model in which LP formulations are expressed through composable primitives for dual objective evaluation and blockwise projection operators for decomposable constraint families. This design allows new formulations to be added locally while reusing a shared optimization loop, diagnostics, and distributed infrastructure.
  To realize the available parallelism, we develop GPU execution techniques tailored to sparse matching constraints, including constraint-aligned sparse layouts, batched projection kernels, and a distributed design that communicates only dual variables. Further, we improve the underlying ridge-regularized dual ascent method with Jacobi-style row normalization, primal scaling, and a continuation scheme for the regularization parameter.
  On extreme-scale matching workloads, the GPU implementation achieves at least a 10x wall-clock speedup over the prior distributed CPU DuaLip solver under matched stopping criteria, while maintaining convergence guarantees.

</details>


### [64] [SLO-Aware Compute Resource Allocation for Prefill-Decode Disaggregated LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04716)
*Luchang Li,Dongfang Li,Bozhao Gong,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM推理中P/D分解的硬件资源优化配置问题，提出了一种结合理论建模与经验基准测试的混合方法。核心贡献包括：1）建立基于吞吐量、输入输出长度及预填充/解码吞吐量的理论模型；2）利用M/M/1排队论建模预填充过程，从最大吞吐量和TTFT推导实际预填充吞吐量；3）通过确定满足TPOT要求的解码批次大小并进行经验测量获得解码吞吐量。实验验证了该方法在真实LLM推理场景中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏一种成熟的方法来确定P/D（预填充-解码）分解的最佳硬件资源数量，同时满足总吞吐量、服务水平目标（SLO）和请求特征（输入和输出长度）的约束。

Method: 1. Theoretical model for calculating P/D resource counts based on total throughput requirements, request input/output lengths, and prefill/decode throughput; 2. M/M/1 queuing theory for modeling prefill process to derive achieved prefill throughput from maximum throughput and TTFT; 3. Empirical measurement of decode throughput by determining decode batch sizes meeting TPOT requirements

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的方法可以准确预测实际LLM推理场景中的最佳P/D资源分配。

Conclusion: The proposed hybrid approach combining theoretical modeling with empirical benchmarking can accurately predict optimal P/D resource allocation in real-world LLM inference scenarios.

Abstract: Prefill-Decode (P/D) disaggregation has emerged as a widely adopted optimization strategy for Large Language Model (LLM) inference. However, there currently exists no well-established methodology for determining the optimal number of P/D hardware resources, subject to constraints on total throughput, service level objectives (SLOs), and request characteristics - specifically input and output lengths. To address this gap, we propose a hybrid approach that combines theoretical modeling with empirical benchmarking. First, we present a theoretical model for calculating P/D resource counts, which is based on total throughput requirements, request input and output lengths, as well as prefill and decode throughput. Then, to obtain the actual prefill and decode throughput under SLO constraints, we model the prefill process using M/M/1 queuing theory, deriving the achieved prefill throughput from the benchmarked maximum prefill throughput and Time-To-First-Token (TTFT). For the decode phase, we determine the decode batch sizes that meet Time-Per-Output-Token (TPOT) requirements and obtain the corresponding decode throughput through empirical measurements. Our experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method can accurately predict optimal P/D resource allocation in real-world LLM inference scenarios.

</details>


### [65] [The Semantic Arrow of Time, Part III: RDMA and the Completion Fallacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04774)
*Paul Borrill*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文是"时间的语义箭头"系列论文的第三篇，研究工业级RDMA系统中违背语义箭头原则的后果。作者提出"完成谬误"概念：RDMA完成语义只保证数据放置而非语义承诺，并通过对Meta、Google、Microsoft的案例研究以及与CXL 3.0、NVLink、UALink的对比分析，证明只有包含强制反射阶段的协议架构才能弥合交付与承诺之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前两篇论文已识别出计算中隐藏的语义箭头时间和FITO范畴错误，并提出OAE链接状态机的建设性替代方案。本文旨在研究当这些原则在工业规模上被违背时会发生什么，选取RDMA作为研究对象，因其是部署在Meta、Google、Microsoft等大型数据中心的高性能数据移动技术。

Method: 1) 提出"完成谬误"概念，区分数据放置(placement)与语义承诺(commitment)；2) 通过RDMA写操作的七个时间阶段形式化文档化该谬误；3) 追踪四个案例研究：Meta的RoCE fabric、Google的1RMA重新设计、Microsoft的DCQCN失败、SDR-RDMA部分完成；4) 对比分析CXL 3.0、NVLink和UALink的解决方案。

Result: 1) 证明RDMA完成信号与应用语义满足之间的差距可以任意大；2) 现有协议(CXL 3.0、NVLink、UALink)均只能部分解决完成谬误，无法完全消除；3) 只有包含强制反射阶段的协议架构才能完全弥合交付与承诺之间的差距。

Conclusion: 完成谬误是RDMA等高性能数据移动技术中的核心问题，其根源在于缺乏语义箭头时间框架。未来的高性能互连协议设计需要纳入强制反射阶段，以確保数据不仅被放置，更被应用语义真正承诺。

Abstract: This is the third of five papers comprising The Semantic Arrow of Time. Parts I and II identified computing's hidden semantic arrow of time, the FITO category mistake, and presented the constructive alternative: the OAE link state machine with its mandatory reflecting phase.
  This paper examines what happens when those principles are violated at industrial scale. Remote Direct Memory Access (RDMA) is the highest-performance data movement technology in production, deployed across Meta's 24,000-GPU clusters, Google's data centers, and Microsoft's Azure infrastructure. We argue that RDMA's completion semantics contain a category mistake: they guarantee placement (data written to a remote NIC buffer) but not commitment (data semantically integrated by the receiving application). We call this the completion fallacy.
  We document the fallacy through seven temporal stages of an RDMA Write operation, showing that the gap between completion signal and application semantic satisfaction can be arbitrarily large. We trace consequences through four case studies: Meta's RoCE fabric, Google's 1RMA redesign, Microsoft's DCQCN failures, and SDR-RDMA partial completions.
  A comparative analysis shows CXL 3.0, NVLink, and UALink each address parts of the completion fallacy but none eliminates it entirely. Only a protocol architecture with a mandatory reflecting phase can close the gap between delivery and commitment.

</details>


### [66] [Unlocking Python's Cores: Hardware Usage and Energy Implications of Removing the GIL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04782)
*José Daniel Montoya Salazar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 研究Python 3.14.2无GIL构建的性能、能耗和资源利用率，对比4类工作负载发现：可并行工作负载可获4倍加速和能效提升，但顺序工作负载能耗反增13-43%，内存使用普遍增加。无GIL非普适优化，需根据工作负载特性选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究关注GIL禁用对速度的影响，但缺乏对能耗和硬件利用率的系统评估。

Method: 使用4类工作负载（NumPy计算、顺序内核、线程化数值工作负载、线程化对象工作负载），对比Python 3.14.2的GIL和free-threaded构建，测量执行时间、CPU利用率、内存使用和能耗。

Result: 可并行工作负载：执行时间最多减少4倍，能耗按比例下降，多核利用有效，内存增加；顺序工作负载：能耗增加13-43%；频繁对象竞争工作负载：收益下降或退化；能耗与执行时间成正比；no-GIL构建虚拟内存增加明显。

Conclusion: Python的no-GIL构建并非通用改进。对于可并行化的工作负载（操作独立数据），可获得显著性能提升（执行时间减少最多4倍）和能效改善；但顺序工作负载反而增加能耗（13-43%），频繁对象访问修改的工作负载因锁竞争而收益下降或退化。内存使用普遍增加，主要源于per-object locking、线程安全机制和新内存分配器。开发者采用前需评估工作负载特性。

Abstract: Python's Global Interpreter Lock prevents execution on more than one CPU core at the same time, even when multiple threads are used. However, starting with Python 3.13 an experimental build allows disabling the GIL. While prior work has examined speedup implications of this disabling, the effects on energy consumption and hardware utilization have received less attention. This study measures execution time, CPU utilization, memory usage, and energy consumption using four workload categories: NumPy-based, sequential kernels, threaded numerical workloads, and threaded object workloads, comparing GIL and free-threaded builds of Python 3.14.2.
  The results highlight a trade-off. For parallelizable workloads operating on independent data, the free-threaded build reduces execution time by up to 4 times, with a proportional reduction in energy consumption, and effective multi-core utilization, at the cost of an increase in memory usage. In contrast, sequential workloads do not benefit from removing the GIL and instead show a 13-43% increase in energy consumption. Similarly, workloads where threads frequently access and modify the same objects show reduced improvements or even degradation due to lock contention. Across all workloads, energy consumption is proportional to execution time, indicating that disabling the GIL does not significantly affect power consumption, even when CPU utilization increases. When it comes to memory, the no-GIL build shows a general increase, more visible in virtual memory than in physical memory. This increase is primarily attributed to per-object locking, additional thread-safety mechanisms in the runtime, and the adoption of a new memory allocator.
  These findings suggest that Python's no-GIL build is not a universal improvement. Developers should evaluate whether their workload can effectively benefit from parallel execution before adoption.

</details>


### [67] [The Semantic Arrow of Time, Part IV: Why Transactions Fail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04810)
*Paul Borrill*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文是"时间的语义箭头"系列论文的第四部分，探讨前向时间假设在文件系统同步、电子邮件和记忆系统中导致的意义破坏问题。作者认为FIT0范畴错误（将信息的前向流动误认为语义排序）在不同规模（NIC缓冲区、云文件、收件箱、海马体、transformer）中反复出现，并提出语义箭头时间原则：系统不能仅依赖信息的前向流动来判断语义整合成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算系统中隐藏的时间箭头被假设为热力学箭头，但作者认为是语义的。当前系统普遍存在一个结构性问题：系统在没有"反映阶段"的情况下前向提交状态变化，导致无法区分成功的语义整合与单纯的时间连续性。这一问题在数据中心的RDMA中已存在，现在需要追溯到用户日常使用的系统中。

Method: 通过分析三个具体领域识别共同的结构模式：1) 文件同步（云平台因last-writer-wins无法表示分布式因果而静默删除用户内容）；2) 电子邮件（基于时间戳的排序导致幻象消息、因果违反和同步停滞）；3) 记忆（人和机器的记忆重建过程缺乏事务保证导致系统性语义腐败）。

Result: 在三个领域中发现了相同的结构模式：系统在没有反映阶段的情况下前向提交状态变化，无法区分语义整合成功与单纯的时间继承。这个模式在不同尺度上一致出现：字节、文件、消息、记忆痕迹、token。

Conclusion: 语义箭头时间原则被违反的条件是：系统将信息的前向流动视为意义的充分证据。第五部分将展示Leibniz Bridge如何提供一个统一框架来弥合这五个领域中的差距。

Abstract: This is the fourth of five papers comprising The Semantic Arrow of Time. Parts I-III established that computing's hidden arrow of time is semantic rather than thermodynamic, that bilateral transaction protocols create causal order through a mandatory reflecting phase, and that RDMA's completion semantics implement the FITO category mistake at industrial scale.
  This paper traces the consequences of the FITO category mistake beyond the data center, into systems people use every day. We examine three domains where forward-only temporal assumptions destroy meaning: file synchronization, where cloud platforms silently delete user content because last-writer-wins cannot represent distributed causality; email, where timestamp-based ordering produces phantom messages, causality violations, and stuck synchronization; and memory--both human and artificial--where reconstructive processes that operate without transactional guarantees produce systematic semantic corruption.
  In each domain, we identify the same structural pattern: a system that commits state changes forward in time without a reflecting phase, and that therefore cannot distinguish between successful semantic integration and mere temporal succession. The pattern is not coincidental. It is the FITO category mistake operating at different scales: bytes in a NIC buffer, files in a cloud, messages in an inbox, engrams in a hippocampus, tokens in a transformer.
  We conclude that the semantic arrow of time is violated whenever a system treats the forward flow of information as sufficient evidence of meaning. Part V will show how the Leibniz Bridge provides a unified framework for closing this gap across all five domains.

</details>


### [68] [PromptTuner: SLO-Aware Elastic System for LLM Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05087)
*Wei Gao,Peng Sun,Dmitrii Ustiugov,Tianwei Zhang,Yonggang Wen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出PromptTuner，一个针对大语言模型提示调优的SLO感知弹性系统，旨在优化LLM提示调优性能，在满足服务等级目标的同时降低资源调配成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示调优已成为提升LLM在下游任务性能的主流策略IT企业提供Prompt-Tuning-as-a-Service服务，需在满足用户SLO的同时降低资源成本。现有深度学习资源管理系统不足以优化这些目标。

Method: 提出两个创新点：(1)设计Prompt Bank（提示库）识别高效初始提示以加速提示调优收敛；(2)开发Workload Scheduler（工作负载调度器）实现快速资源分配以减少SLO违规和资源成本。

Result: 评估表明，PromptTuner相比INFless和ElasticFlow分别降低SLO违规4.0倍和7.9倍，降低成本1.6倍和4.5倍。

Conclusion: PromptTuner有效实现了SLO感知的LLM提示调优优化，在减少SLO违规和降低成本方面显著优于现有方案。

Abstract: Prompt tuning has become a prominent strategy for enhancing the performance of Large Language Models (LLMs) on downstream tasks. Many IT enterprises now offer Prompt-Tuning-as-a-Service to fulfill the growing demand for prompt tuning LLMs on downstream tasks. Their primary objective is to satisfy users Service Level Objectives (SLOs) while reducing resource provisioning costs. Nevertheless, our characterization analysis for existing deep learning resource management systems reveals that they are insufficient to optimize these objectives for LLM prompt tuning workloads.
  In this paper, we introduce PromptTuner, an SLO-aware elastic system to optimize LLM prompt tuning. It contains two innovations. (1) We design a Prompt Bank to identify efficient initial prompts to expedite the convergence of prompt tuning. (2) We develop aWorkload Scheduler to enable fast resource allocation to reduce the SLO violation and resource costs. In our evaluation, PromptTuner reduces SLO violations by 4.0x and 7.9x, and lowers costs by 1.6x and 4.5x, compared to INFless and ElasticFlow respectively.

</details>


### [69] [Radiation Hydrodynamics at Scale: Comparing MPI and Asynchronous Many-Task Runtimes with FleCSI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05366)
*Alexander Strack,Hartmut Kaiser,Dirk Pflüger*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文评估了FleCSI框架三种并行后端（MPI、Legion、HPX）的性能。研究表明MPI后端效率最高，HPX在计算密集型应用中小规模下优于MPI，Legion存在明显开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发高效的分布式代码仍然是劳动密集型且复杂的任务。FleCSI框架旨在通过提供基于任务运行时的用户导向高层编程接口来简化应用开发，并集成MPI、Legion和HPX等先进的并行化后端。

Method: 使用FleCSI的三个后端（MPI、Legion、HPX）在最多1024个节点上对两个代表性应用进行基准测试：简单的泊松求解器和多维辐射流体动力学代码HARD。

Result: MPI后端在泊松求解器上实现超过97%的并行效率（弱扩展至131072核心）。Legion后端存在明显开销和扩展限制。HPX后端相比MPI+Kokkos仅引入边际开销，但在少于64个节点时HPX优于MPI+Kokkos（弱扩展平均加速1.31倍，强扩展1.27倍）。纯流体动力学HARD基准中，HPX在少于32个节点时性能更优（相对MPI加速1.20倍，相对MPI+Kokkos加速1.64倍）。

Conclusion: FleCSI的抽象层对MPI后端仅引入最小开销。HPX后端在计算密集型工作负载中表现优于MPI，但可扩展性受限于HPX集体操作未优化；Legion后端存在明显开销和扩展性问题。

Abstract: Writing efficient distributed code remains a labor-intensive and complex endeavor. To simplify application development, the Flexible Computational Science Infrastructure (FleCSI) framework offers a user-oriented, high-level programming interface that is built upon a task-based runtime model. Internally, FleCSI integrates state-of-the-art parallelization backends, including MPI and the asynchronous many-task runtimes (AMTRs) Legion and HPX, enabling applications to fully leverage asynchronous parallelism. In this work, we benchmark two applications using FleCSI's three backends on up to 1024 nodes, intending to quantify the advantages and overheads introduced by the AMTR backends. As representative applications, we select a simple Poisson solver and the multidimensional radiation hydrodynamics code HARD. In the communication-focused Poisson solver benchmark, FleCSI achieves over 97% parallel efficiency using the MPI backend under weak scaling on up to 131072 cores, indicating that only minimal overhead is introduced by its abstraction layer. While the Legion backend exhibits notable overheads and scaling limitations, the HPX backend introduces only marginal overhead compared to MPI+Kokkos. However, the scalability of the HPX backend is currently limited due to the usage of non-optimized HPX collective operations. In the computation-focused radiation hydrodynamics benchmarks, the performance gap between the MPI and HPX backends fades. On fewer than 64 nodes, the HPX backend outperforms MPI+Kokkos, achieving an average speedup of 1.31 under weak scaling and up to 1.27 under strong scaling. For the hydrodynamics-only HARD benchmark, the HPX backend demonstrates superior performance on fewer than 32 nodes, achieving speedups of up to 1.20 relative to MPI and up to 1.64 relative to MPI+Kokkos.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [70] [Decorrelating the Future: Joint Frequency Domain Learning for Spatio-temporal Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04418)
*Zepu Wang,Bowen Liao,Jeff,Ban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FreST Loss，一种频率增强的时空训练目标，通过联合傅里叶变换（JFT）在联合时空频谱域进行监督，解决现有方法忽略空间和跨时空交互的问题，理论分析表明该方法可降低时域训练目标的估计偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标准直接预测模型（如基于MSE的模型）无法捕捉图结构信号中复杂的时空依赖性；现有频域方法（如FreDF）虽然缓解了时间自相关问题，但忽略了空间和跨时空交互作用。

Method: 提出FreST Loss（频率增强的时空训练目标），利用联合傅里叶变换（JFT）将模型预测与真实值在统一频谱域中对齐，将监督扩展到联合时空频谱。

Result: FreST Loss在六个真实世界数据集上持续提升基线模型性能，证明其能更好地捕捉整体时空动态。

Conclusion: FreST Loss is model-agnostic，能够一致性地改进最先进基线模型，更好地捕捉整体时空动态。通过在六个真实世界数据集上的广泛实验验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Standard direct forecasting models typically rely on point-wise objectives such as Mean Squared Error, which fail to capture the complex spatio-temporal dependencies inherent in graph-structured signals. While recent frequency-domain approaches such as FreDF mitigate temporal autocorrelation, they often overlook spatial and cross spatio-temporal interactions. To address this limitation, we propose FreST Loss, a frequency-enhanced spatio-temporal training objective that extends supervision to the joint spatio-temporal spectrum. By leveraging the Joint Fourier Transform (JFT), FreST Loss aligns model predictions with ground truth in a unified spectral domain, effectively decorrelating complex dependencies across both space and time. Theoretical analysis shows that this formulation reduces estimation bias associated with time-domain training objectives. Extensive experiments on six real-world datasets demonstrate that FreST Loss is model-agnostic and consistently improves state-of-the-art baselines by better capturing holistic spatio-temporal dynamics.

</details>


### [71] [Machine Learning for Complex Systems Dynamics: Detecting Bifurcations in Dynamical Systems with Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04420)
*Swadesh Pal,Roderick Melnik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: A novel deep neural network method (EINNs) that reverses traditional bifurcation analysis by learning to map equilibrium states to system parameters, enabling efficient detection of critical thresholds and catastrophic regime shifts in complex dynamical systems.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Critical transitions (abrupt shifts between qualitatively different states) are crucial for understanding tipping points in complex dynamical systems across ecology, climate science, and biology. Traditional detection methods (forward simulations, bifurcation analyses) are computationally intensive and limited by parameter sampling, necessitating a more efficient approach.

Method: The paper proposes equilibrium-informed neural networks (EINNs), a novel machine learning approach that reverses the traditional parameter-search process. Instead of fixing parameters and searching for solutions, EINN uses candidate equilibrium states as inputs and trains a deep neural network (DNN) to infer corresponding system parameters that satisfy equilibrium conditions. Critical thresholds are detected by analyzing the learned parameter landscape for abrupt changes in feasibility or continuity of equilibrium mappings.

Result: The method was demonstrated on nonlinear systems exhibiting saddle-node bifurcations and multi-stability, successfully recovering parameter regions associated with impending transitions.

Conclusion: EINNs provide a flexible alternative to traditional bifurcation analysis techniques, offering new insights into early detection and structure of critical shifts in high-dimensional and nonlinear systems.

Abstract: Critical transitions are the abrupt shifts between qualitatively different states of a system, and they are crucial to understanding tipping points in complex dynamical systems across ecology, climate science, and biology. Detecting these shifts typically involves extensive forward simulations or bifurcation analyses, which are often computationally intensive and limited by parameter sampling. In this study, we propose a novel machine learning approach based on deep neural networks (DNNs) called equilibrium-informed neural networks (EINNs) to identify critical thresholds associated with catastrophic regime shifts. Rather than fixing parameters and searching for solutions, the EINN method reverses this process by using candidate equilibrium states as inputs and training a DNN to infer the corresponding system parameters that satisfy the equilibrium condition. By analyzing the learned parameter landscape and observing abrupt changes in the feasibility or continuity of equilibrium mappings, critical thresholds can be effectively detected. We demonstrate this capability on nonlinear systems exhibiting saddle-node bifurcations and multi-stability, showing that EINNs can recover the parameter regions associated with impending transitions. This method provides a flexible alternative to traditional techniques, offering new insights into the early detection and structure of critical shifts in high-dimensional and nonlinear systems.

</details>


### [72] [Delta-Crosscoder: Robust Crosscoder Model Diffing in Narrow Fine-Tuning Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04426)
*Aly Kassem,Thomas Jiralerspong,Negar Rostamzadeh,Golnoosh Farnadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Delta-Crosscoder是一种结合BatchTopK稀疏性、delta损失和对比信号的新型跨编码器方法，用于识别微调模型中导致行为变化的潜在方向，在10个模型任务上表现优于SAE基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨编码器方法在处理窄范围微调时存在困难，因为行为变化通常是局部的且不对称的。现有公式难以有效识别这种局部化的变化。

Method: Delta-Crosscoder结合三种技术：(1) BatchTopK稀疏性实现稀疏激活；(2) delta损失优先关注两个模型之间发生改变的方向；(3) 来自配对激活的隐式对比信号。评估涵盖10种模型任务，包括合成虚假事实、突发错位、潜意识学习和禁忌词猜测，使用Gemma、LLaMA、Qwen模型（1B-9B参数）。

Result: Delta-Crosscoder能够可靠地隔离出对微调行为负责的潜在方向，并实现有效的缓解。在10个模型任务中，该方法优于基于SAE的基线方法，同时与基于Non-SAE的方法表现相当。

Conclusion: 跨编码器仍然是模型差异分析的有效工具。Delta-Crosscoder通过专门设计处理窄范围微调的非对称和局部特性，成功解决了之前方法的局限性。

Abstract: Model diffing methods aim to identify how fine-tuning changes a model's internal representations. Crosscoders approach this by learning shared dictionaries of interpretable latent directions between base and fine-tuned models. However, existing formulations struggle with narrow fine-tuning, where behavioral changes are localized and asymmetric. We introduce Delta-Crosscoder, which combines BatchTopK sparsity with a delta-based loss prioritizing directions that change between models, plus an implicit contrastive signal from paired activations on matched inputs. Evaluated across 10 model organisms, including synthetic false facts, emergent misalignment, subliminal learning, and taboo word guessing (Gemma, LLaMA, Qwen; 1B-9B parameters), Delta-Crosscoder reliably isolates latent directions causally responsible for fine-tuned behaviors and enables effective mitigation, outperforming SAE-based baselines, while matching the Non-SAE-based. Our results demonstrate that crosscoders remain a powerful tool for model diffing.

</details>


### [73] [Thin Keys, Full Values: Reducing KV Cache via Low-Dimensional Attention Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04427)
*Hengshuai Yao,Guan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非对称注意力机制，认为标准Transformer中查询/键用于"选择"操作（低维），而值用于"价值传递"（高维），因此可以采用不同的维度配置。实验表明该方法可在减少75% QK参数的同时，仅增加4.3%的困惑度；在7B模型上实现75%键缓存节省，代价仅2%质量损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
